  <script>
    // =========================================================================
    // KHỞI TẠO BIẾN TRẠNG THÁI TOÀN CỤC
    // =========================================================================
    let currentStream = null;
    let videoTrack = null;
    let facingMode = "environment";
    let isGrading = false;
    let opticalInterval = null;
    let lock4Frames = 0;
    let deferredPrompt = null;
    let latestSubId = null;
    let isTorchOn = false;
    let toastTimer = null;
    let sessionRetryTimer = null;

    let sessionData = {
      active_exam_id: null,
      exam_info: null,
      exam_data: null,
      submissions: [],
      all_exams: []
    };

    const RETICLES = [
      { id: "reticle-tl", name: "Trên-Trái", matched: false },
      { id: "reticle-tr", name: "Trên-Phải", matched: false },
      { id: "reticle-bl", name: "Dưới-Trái", matched: false },
      { id: "reticle-br", name: "Dưới-Phải", matched: false }
    ];

    // Phát âm thanh phản hồi bằng Web Audio API
    function playBeep(isSuccess = true) {
      try {
        const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
        if (!AudioContext) return;
        const ctx = new AudioContext();
        const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
        const gain = ctx.createGain();
        osc.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(ctx.destination);
        
        if (isSuccess) {
          osc.type = "sine";
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(587.33, ctx.currentTime); // D5
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(880, ctx.currentTime + 0.08); // A5
          gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.25, ctx.currentTime);
          gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, ctx.currentTime + 0.25);
          osc.start(ctx.currentTime);
          osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.25);
        } else {
          osc.type = "triangle";
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(220, ctx.currentTime);
          gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.2, ctx.currentTime);
          gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, ctx.currentTime + 0.2);
          osc.start(ctx.currentTime);
          osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.2);
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    }

    // Hiển thị thông báo Toast không gây đơ camera
    function showNotificationToast(msg, isError = true) {
      const toast = document.getElementById("floatingToast");
      const content = document.getElementById("floatingToastContent");
      const icon = document.getElementById("toastIcon");
      const text = document.getElementById("toastMsg");

      if (isError) {
        content.className = "bg-rose-950/95 border border-rose-500 text-rose-200 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl backdrop-blur text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-2 max-w-sm text-center";
        icon.innerText = "⚠️";
      } else {
        content.className = "bg-emerald-950/95 border border-emerald-500 text-emerald-200 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl backdrop-blur text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-2 max-w-sm text-center";
        icon.innerText = "✓";
      }
      text.innerText = msg;

      toast.classList.remove("-translate-y-12", "opacity-0");
      toast.classList.add("translate-y-0", "opacity-100");

      if (toastTimer) clearTimeout(toastTimer);
      toastTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        toast.classList.remove("translate-y-0", "opacity-100");
        toast.classList.add("-translate-y-12", "opacity-0");
      }, 3500);
    }

    // Kiểm tra nếu đang mở trong trình duyệt nhúng của ứng dụng (Zalo / FB / Messenger)
    const ua = navigator.userAgent || "";
    if (/Zalo|FBAN|FBAV|Instagram|Messenger|Line/i.test(ua)) {
      const alertEl = document.getElementById("inAppBrowserAlert");
      if (alertEl) alertEl.classList.remove("hidden");
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // PWA & SERVICE WORKER REGISTRATION
    // =========================================================================
    if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
      window.addEventListener('load', () => {
        navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js').catch(err => console.log('SW Fail:', err));
      });
    }

    window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      deferredPrompt = e;
      const btn = document.getElementById("btnPwaInstall");
      if (btn) btn.classList.remove("hidden");
    });

    function promptPwaInstall() {
      if (deferredPrompt) {
        deferredPrompt.prompt();
        deferredPrompt.userChoice.then(() => {
          deferredPrompt = null;
          document.getElementById("btnPwaInstall").classList.add("hidden");
        });
      } else {
        alert("Để cài App:\n- Trên iPhone (Safari): Bấm nút Chia Sẻ (Share ⬆️) -> Chọn 'Thêm vào MH chính'.\n- Trên Android: Bấm menu 3 chấm (⋮) -> Chọn 'Cài đặt ứng dụng'.");
      }
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // KHỞI ĐỘNG ỨNG DỤNG (BẬT CAMERA NGAY LẬP TỨC TRONG 200MS, KHÔNG CHỜ SERVER)
    // =========================================================================
    window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
      // 1. Mở Camera tức thì, không bị chặn bởi mạng
      startCamera();

      // 2. Tải dữ liệu đợt thi ngầm với cơ chế tự động thử lại khi máy chủ Render khởi động
      fetchSessionWithRetry();

      // 3. Khởi động vòng lặp kiểm tra mốc quang học 90ms/chu kỳ
      opticalInterval = setInterval(runOpticalMarkerDetection, 90);

      // Chạm màn hình để bật video nếu bị chặn autoplay
      document.body.addEventListener("click", () => {
        const video = document.getElementById("videoElement");
        if (video && video.paused) {
          video.play().catch(e => {});
        }
      }, { once: true });
    });

    // =========================================================================
    // ĐIỀU HƯỚNG TABS
    // =========================================================================
    function switchTab(tabId) {
      document.querySelectorAll(".tab-content").forEach(el => el.classList.remove("active"));
      document.getElementById(tabId).classList.add("active");

      const navs = {
        tabScanner: document.getElementById("navScanner"),
        tabResults: document.getElementById("navResults"),
        tabExam: document.getElementById("navExam")
      };

      Object.keys(navs).forEach(k => {
        if (k === tabId) {
          navs[k].className = "flex flex-col items-center gap-0.5 text-emerald-400 font-bold text-[10px]";
        } else {
          navs[k].className = "flex flex-col items-center gap-0.5 text-slate-400 font-semibold text-[10px]";
        }
      });

      if (tabId === "tabScanner") {
        if (!currentStream) startCamera();
        if (!opticalInterval) opticalInterval = setInterval(runOpticalMarkerDetection, 90);
      } else {
        if (opticalInterval) { clearInterval(opticalInterval); opticalInterval = null; }
        if (tabId === "tabResults") renderSubmissionsList();
        if (tabId === "tabExam") renderExamsList();
      }
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // ĐỒNG BỘ DỮ LIỆU PHIÊN CHẤM VỚI CƠ CHẾ AUTO-RETRY KHI SERVER COLD-START
    // =========================================================================
    async function fetchSessionWithRetry() {
      const dot = document.getElementById("serverStatusDot");
      const statusText = document.getElementById("serverStatusText");

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/session");
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error("HTTP " + res.status);
        
        sessionData = await res.json();
        
        const count = sessionData.submissions ? sessionData.submissions.length : 0;
        document.getElementById("badgeSubCount").innerText = `${count} bài`;

        if (sessionData.exam_info) {
          const title = `${sessionData.exam_info.subject} - LỚP ${sessionData.exam_info.class_name}`;
          document.getElementById("headerExamTitle").innerText = title;
          document.getElementById("headerExamSubtitle").innerText = `Học kỳ: ${sessionData.exam_info.period}`;

          document.getElementById("cardExamSubject").innerText = `${sessionData.exam_info.subject} (${sessionData.exam_info.class_name})`;
          document.getElementById("cardExamDetails").innerText = `Kỳ thi: ${sessionData.exam_info.period} | Năm học: ${sessionData.exam_info.school_year}`;
        }

        const hasKeys = sessionData.exam_info && sessionData.exam_info.is_configured;
        const statusBadge = document.getElementById("cardAnswerStatus");
        if (hasKeys) {
          statusBadge.innerText = "✓ Đã nạp đáp án";
          statusBadge.className = "px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-800";
        } else {
          statusBadge.innerText = "Chưa có đáp án";
          statusBadge.className = "px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold bg-amber-950 text-amber-300 border border-amber-800";
        }

        if (sessionRetryTimer) {
          clearTimeout(sessionRetryTimer);
          sessionRetryTimer = null;
        }

      } catch (err) {
        if (dot) dot.className = "w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-400 animate-ping";
        if (statusText) statusText.innerText = "Đang kết nối lại máy chủ...";
        sessionRetryTimer = setTimeout(fetchSessionWithRetry, 3500);
      }
    }

    async function refreshSessionData() {
      await fetchSessionWithRetry();
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // CAMERA & NHẬN DIỆN MỐC QUANG HỌC CHUẨN XÁC
    // =========================================================================
    async function startCamera() {
      if (currentStream) {
        try {
          currentStream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
        } catch(e) {}
        currentStream = null;
      }

      const video = document.getElementById("videoElement");
      video.muted = true;
      video.setAttribute("autoplay", "");
      video.setAttribute("playsinline", "");
      video.setAttribute("muted", "");

      const constraintCandidates = [
        { video: { facingMode: { ideal: facingMode }, width: { ideal: 1920 }, height: { ideal: 1080 } }, audio: false },
        { video: { facingMode: { ideal: facingMode }, width: { ideal: 1280 }, height: { ideal: 720 } }, audio: false },
        { video: { facingMode: facingMode }, audio: false },
        { video: true, audio: false }
      ];

      let stream = null;
      let lastErr = null;

      for (const c of constraintCandidates) {
        try {
          stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia(c);
          if (stream) break;
        } catch (e) {
          lastErr = e;
        }
      }

      if (!stream) {
        console.warn("Camera getUserMedia failed:", lastErr);
        showNotificationToast("Không mở được camera trực tiếp. Hãy bấm 'Chụp máy gốc'!", true);
        return;
      }

      try {
        currentStream = stream;
        video.srcObject = stream;
        videoTrack = stream.getVideoTracks()[0];

        video.onloadedmetadata = async () => {
          try {
            await video.play();
          } catch (e) {
            console.warn("Video play error on metadata:", e);
          }
        };

        await video.play();
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn("Video play initial warning:", e);
      }
    }

    function switchCamera() {
      facingMode = (facingMode === "environment") ? "user" : "environment";
      startCamera();
    }

    async function toggleTorch() {
      if (videoTrack && videoTrack.getCapabilities && videoTrack.getCapabilities().torch) {
        try {
          isTorchOn = !isTorchOn;
          await videoTrack.applyConstraints({ advanced: [{ torch: isTorchOn }] });
          document.getElementById("torchLabel").innerText = isTorchOn ? "Tắt đèn" : "Bật đèn";
        } catch (e) {
          showNotificationToast("Không thể điều khiển đèn Flash: " + e.message, true);
        }
      } else {
        showNotificationToast("Thiết bị không hỗ trợ bật đèn Flash trực tiếp.", true);
      }
    }

    // Thuật toán quang học quét mốc đen chuẩn xác (chỉ nhận diện trên nền giấy trắng, chống bắt nhầm màn hình đen)
    function runOpticalMarkerDetection() {
      if (isGrading) return;
      const video = document.getElementById("videoElement");
      // BẮT BUỘC: Video phải sẵn sàng, không bị tạm dừng và kích thước video > 0
      if (!video || video.readyState < 2 || video.paused || video.videoWidth === 0 || video.videoHeight === 0) return;

      const vw = video.videoWidth;
      const vh = video.videoHeight;
      const cw = video.clientWidth;
      const ch = video.clientHeight;
      if (cw === 0 || ch === 0) return;

      const scale = Math.max(cw / vw, ch / vh);
      const offsetX = (cw - vw * scale) / 2;
      const offsetY = (ch - vh * scale) / 2;

      const canvas = document.getElementById("analysisCanvas");
      canvas.width = 36;
      canvas.height = 36;
      const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d", { willReadFrequently: true });

      let matchedCount = 0;

      RETICLES.forEach((reticle) => {
        const el = document.getElementById(reticle.id);
        const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();

        const sampleW = Math.round((rect.width / scale) * 1.8);
        const sampleH = Math.round((rect.height / scale) * 1.8);
        const vidX = Math.round((rect.left + rect.width / 2 - offsetX) / scale);
        
        // Hơi nâng nhẹ tâm lấy mẫu cho 2 mốc dưới lên 6px để tâm ngắm nằm chính xác trên mốc M4,
        // tránh trường hợp mốc đen nằm sát viền giấy bị lọt ra ngoài hoặc chạm nền bàn đen
        const offsetYFactor = (reticle.id === "reticle-bl" || reticle.id === "reticle-br") ? -6 : 0;
        const vidY = Math.round((rect.top + rect.height / 2 + offsetYFactor - offsetY) / scale);

        const sx = Math.max(0, Math.min(vw - sampleW, vidX - sampleW / 2));
        const sy = Math.max(0, Math.min(vh - sampleH, vidY - sampleH / 2));

        ctx.drawImage(video, sx, sy, sampleW, sampleH, 0, 0, 36, 36);
        const imgData = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, 36, 36).data;

        let minLum = 255, maxLum = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < imgData.length; i += 4) {
          const lum = 0.299 * imgData[i] + 0.587 * imgData[i + 1] + 0.114 * imgData[i + 2];
          if (lum < minLum) minLum = lum;
          if (lum > maxLum) maxLum = lum;
        }

        const contrast = maxLum - minLum;
        const thresh = minLum + contrast * 0.40;
        let darkPixels = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < imgData.length; i += 4) {
          const lum = 0.299 * imgData[i] + 0.587 * imgData[i + 1] + 0.114 * imgData[i + 2];
          if (lum <= thresh) darkPixels++;
        }

        const darkRatio = darkPixels / (36 * 36);

        // ĐIỀU KIỆN MỐC ĐEN CHÍNH XÁC & BỀN VỮNG:
        // 1. maxLum >= 105: BẮT BUỘC có bề mặt giấy trắng sáng (chống bắt nhầm màn hình đen / bóng tối)
        // 2. contrast >= 22.0: Đủ độ tương phản giữa mốc đen và nền giấy trắng
        // 3. minLum < maxLum * 0.82: Mốc đen tối rõ rệt so với nền giấy
        // 4. darkRatio từ 2% đến 65%: Khớp mốc đen hoàn hảo kể cả khi mép bàn đen hoặc chữ ký lọt một phần vào vùng ngắm
        const isMatched = (maxLum >= 105 && contrast >= 22.0 && minLum < maxLum * 0.82 && darkRatio >= 0.02 && darkRatio <= 0.65);
        reticle.matched = isMatched;

        if (isMatched) {
          el.classList.remove("unmatched");
          el.classList.add("matched");
          matchedCount++;
        } else {
          el.classList.remove("matched");
          el.classList.add("unmatched");
        }
      });

      updateGuidanceUI(matchedCount);
    }

    function updateGuidanceUI(matchedCount) {
      if (isGrading) return;

      const guideBox = document.getElementById("guidanceBox");
      const guideBoxText = document.getElementById("guideBoxText");
      const guideIcon = document.getElementById("guideIcon");
      const guideSubText = document.getElementById("guideText");
      const viewfinder = document.getElementById("viewfinder");

      // CHỈ TỰ ĐỘNG CHỤP KHI ĐÃ NHẬN ĐỦ 4 GÓC (matchedCount === 4) VÀ GIỮ VỮNG TAY 2 CHU KỲ (~180ms)
      if (matchedCount === 4) {
        lock4Frames++;
        viewfinder.className = "relative w-[88%] max-w-[330px] aspect-[1/1.444] border-2 border-emerald-400 rounded-2xl viewfinder-mask shadow-[0_0_28px_rgba(16,185,129,0.9)] transition-all flex flex-col justify-between p-1";
        guideBox.className = "text-center text-[11px] font-bold text-emerald-200 bg-emerald-950/95 px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full mx-auto shadow-xl border border-emerald-500 animate-pulse flex items-center gap-1.5 pointer-events-auto";
        guideIcon.innerText = "✓";
        guideBoxText.innerText = "ĐÃ KHÓA ĐỦ 4 MỐC! ĐANG CHỤP...";
        guideSubText.innerText = "Giữ chắc tay, hệ thống đang tự động chụp và chấm bài...";

        if (lock4Frames >= 2) {
          executeGradingProcess();
        }
        return;
      }

      // NẾU CHƯA ĐỦ 4 GÓC: RESET BỘ ĐẾM, TUYỆT ĐỐI KHÔNG TỰ ĐỘNG CHỤP!
      lock4Frames = 0;

      viewfinder.className = "relative w-[88%] max-w-[330px] aspect-[1/1.444] border-2 border-dashed border-emerald-400/70 rounded-2xl viewfinder-mask transition-all flex flex-col justify-between p-1";
      guideBox.className = "text-center text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-300 bg-black/85 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full mx-auto shadow-xl border border-amber-500/50 transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 pointer-events-auto";

      if (matchedCount === 3) {
        guideIcon.innerText = "🔍";
        guideBoxText.innerText = "Đã khớp 3/4 mốc. Căn nốt mốc còn lại!";
        guideSubText.innerText = "Đã tìm thấy 3 mốc. Đưa nốt góc mốc thứ 4 vào ngàm hoặc bấm 'Chụp và chấm ngay'!";
      } else if (matchedCount === 2) {
        guideIcon.innerText = "📐";
        guideBoxText.innerText = "Đã khớp 2/4 mốc. Giữ phẳng camera!";
        guideSubText.innerText = "Cầm điện thoại song song mặt bàn để khớp cả 4 mốc.";
      } else if (matchedCount === 1) {
        guideIcon.innerText = "🎯";
        guideBoxText.innerText = "Đã khớp 1/4 mốc. Đưa gần hơn!";
        guideSubText.innerText = "Căn chỉnh 4 mốc đen góc vào 4 khung ngắm.";
      } else {
        guideIcon.innerText = "🎯";
        guideBoxText.innerText = "Đưa 4 góc mốc đen vào khung";
        guideSubText.innerText = "Căn 4 mốc đen góc vào 4 khung ngắm. Đủ 4 mốc máy sẽ tự chụp.";
      }
    }

    function triggerManualCapture() {
      if (!isGrading) executeGradingProcess();
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // GỬI ẢNH LÊN MÁY CHỦ CHẤM THI (TỪ VIDEO CANVAS)
    // =========================================================================
    async function executeGradingProcess() {
      if (isGrading) return;
      isGrading = true;
      if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(60);

      const guideBox = document.getElementById("guidanceBox");
      const guideBoxText = document.getElementById("guideBoxText");
      const guideIcon = document.getElementById("guideIcon");
      const guideSubText = document.getElementById("guideText");
      
      guideIcon.innerText = "⏳";
      guideBoxText.innerText = "Đang nắn phẳng & chấm điểm...";
      guideSubText.innerText = "Hệ thống đang đối chiếu đáp án với mã đề bài thi...";
      guideBox.className = "text-center text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-200 bg-amber-950/95 px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full mx-auto shadow-xl border border-amber-500 animate-pulse flex items-center gap-1.5 pointer-events-auto";

      const video = document.getElementById("videoElement");
      const canvas = document.getElementById("captureCanvas");
      canvas.width = video.videoWidth || 1920;
      canvas.height = video.videoHeight || 1080;
      canvas.getContext("2d").drawImage(video, 0, 0);

      canvas.toBlob(async (blob) => {
        if (!blob) {
          isGrading = false;
          resetGuidanceHUD();
          return;
        }
        await postGradeImageBlob(blob, `Mobile_${Date.now()}.jpg`);
      }, "image/jpeg", 0.92);
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // CHỤP BẰNG CAMERA MÁY NATIVE (FAIL-SAFE 100% HOẠT ĐỘNG MỌI ỨNG DỤNG)
    // =========================================================================
    async function handleNativeCameraCapture(event) {
      const file = event.target.files[0];
      if (!file) return;

      isGrading = true;
      if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(60);

      const guideBoxText = document.getElementById("guideBoxText");
      const guideIcon = document.getElementById("guideIcon");
      guideIcon.innerText = "⏳";
      guideBoxText.innerText = "Đang nắn phẳng & chấm điểm...";

      await postGradeImageBlob(file, file.name || `Native_${Date.now()}.jpg`);
      event.target.value = ""; // Reset file input
    }

    async function postGradeImageBlob(blobOrFile, filename) {
      const fd = new FormData();
      fd.append("file", blobOrFile, filename);

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/grade-single-file", { method: "POST", body: fd });
        
        if (!res.ok) {
          const errData = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
          const errDetail = errData.detail || `Lỗi máy chủ (${res.status})`;
          playBeep(false);
          if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate([100, 60, 100]);
          showNotificationToast(errDetail, true);
          return;
        }

        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.new_submissions && data.new_submissions.length > 0) {
          const sub = data.new_submissions[0];
          latestSubId = sub.submission_id;
          if (sub.has_error) {
            playBeep(false);
            if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate([100, 60, 100]);
            showNotificationToast(sub.error_msg || "Ảnh chụp không đạt chuẩn. Vui lòng chụp thẳng và đủ sáng.", true);
            await refreshSessionData();
            renderSubmissionsList();
          } else {
            playBeep(true);
            if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate([60, 40, 120]);
            showResultToast(sub);
            await refreshSessionData();
            renderSubmissionsList();
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        playBeep(false);
        showNotificationToast("Lỗi kết nối máy chủ: " + err.message, true);
      } finally {
        isGrading = false;
        lock4Frames = 0;
        resetGuidanceHUD();
      }
    }

    function resetGuidanceHUD() {
      const guideBox = document.getElementById("guidanceBox");
      const guideBoxText = document.getElementById("guideBoxText");
      const guideIcon = document.getElementById("guideIcon");
      const guideSubText = document.getElementById("guideText");
      if (guideBox && guideBoxText && guideIcon && guideSubText) {
        guideIcon.innerText = "🎯";
        guideBoxText.innerText = "Đưa 4 góc mốc đen vào khung";
        guideSubText.innerText = "Căn 4 mốc đen góc vào 4 khung ngắm. Đủ 4 mốc máy sẽ tự chụp.";
        guideBox.className = "text-center text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-300 bg-black/85 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full mx-auto shadow-xl border border-amber-500/50 transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 pointer-events-auto";
      }
    }

    function showResultToast(sub) {
      latestSubId = sub.submission_id;
      document.getElementById("toastTotalScore").innerText = sub.scores ? sub.scores.total : "0.0";
      document.getElementById("toastSbd").innerText = sub.sbd || "---";
      document.getElementById("toastExamCode").innerText = sub.exam_code || "---";
      
      const s = sub.scores || {};
      document.getElementById("toastBreakdown").innerText = `P1: ${s.p1 || 0} | P2: ${s.p2 || 0} | P3: ${s.p3 || 0}`;

      const drawer = document.getElementById("recentResultDrawer");
      drawer.classList.remove("hidden");
    }

    function closeResultToast() {
      document.getElementById("recentResultDrawer").classList.add("hidden");
    }

    function viewLatestGradedImage() {
      if (latestSubId) {
        openImageViewer(latestSubId, `SBD: ${document.getElementById("toastSbd").innerText}`);
      }
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // HIỂN THỊ DANH SÁCH BÀI LÀM (TAB 2)
    // =========================================================================
    function renderSubmissionsList() {
      const container = document.getElementById("resultsListContainer");
      const subs = sessionData.submissions || [];
      const query = (document.getElementById("searchSbdInput").value || "").trim().toLowerCase();

      const filtered = subs.filter(s => {
        const sbd = (s.sbd || "").toLowerCase();
        const code = (s.exam_code || "").toLowerCase();
        return !query || sbd.includes(query) || code.includes(query);
      });

      if (filtered.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = `
          <div class="text-center text-slate-500 py-16 text-xs">
            ${query ? "Không tìm thấy bài thi khớp số báo danh này." : "Chưa có bài thi nào được chấm trong đợt này."}
          </div>
        `;
        return;
      }

      container.innerHTML = filtered.map(sub => {
        const isErr = sub.has_error;
        const total = sub.scores ? sub.scores.total : 0.0;
        return `
          <div onclick="openImageViewer('${sub.submission_id}', 'SBD: ${sub.sbd || 'Chưa rõ'} - Đề: ${sub.exam_code || '---'}')" class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-3 flex justify-between items-center active:scale-[0.99] transition cursor-pointer">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div class="w-10 h-10 ${isErr ? 'bg-rose-950 border-rose-800 text-rose-300' : 'bg-indigo-950 border-indigo-800 text-emerald-400'} border rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center font-black text-sm">
                ${isErr ? '!' : total}
              </div>
              <div>
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span class="text-xs font-bold text-white">SBD: ${sub.sbd || 'Chưa rõ'}</span>
                  <span class="text-[9px] bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded font-mono">Đề: ${sub.exam_code || '---'}</span>
                </div>
                <p class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5 truncate max-w-[180px]">
                  ${isErr ? (sub.error_msg || 'Lỗi nhận diện') : `P1: ${sub.scores.p1} | P2: ${sub.scores.p2} | P3: ${sub.scores.p3}`}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2" onclick="event.stopPropagation()">
              <button onclick="openImageViewer('${sub.submission_id}', 'SBD: ${sub.sbd || 'Chưa rõ'}')" class="p-1.5 text-indigo-400 hover:text-indigo-300 text-xs" title="Xem chi tiết">
                🔍
              </button>
              <button onclick="deleteSubmissionMobile('${sub.submission_id}')" class="p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:text-rose-400 text-xs" title="Xóa bài">
                🗑️
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }).join("");
    }

    async function refreshSubmissions() {
      await refreshSessionData();
      renderSubmissionsList();
    }

    async function deleteSubmissionMobile(subId) {
      if (!confirm("Bạn có chắc chắn muốn xóa bài thi này?")) return;
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/delete-submission/${subId}`, { method: "DELETE" });
        if (res.ok) {
          await refreshSessionData();
          renderSubmissionsList();
        }
      } catch (e) {
        showNotificationToast("Lỗi khi xóa bài: " + e.message, true);
      }
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // XEM ẢNH BÀI CHẤM TOÀN MÀN HÌNH (TỰ ĐỘNG FALLBACK CẢ 2 ROUTE)
    // =========================================================================
    function openImageViewer(subId, title) {
      document.getElementById("viewerTitle").innerText = title;
      document.getElementById("viewerSubtitle").innerText = `Mã bài: ${subId}`;
      const img = document.getElementById("viewerImage");
      const loading = document.getElementById("viewerLoading");
      
      if (loading) {
        loading.classList.remove("hidden");
        loading.innerHTML = `
          <svg class="animate-spin w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400 inline" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle><path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8v8H4z"></path></svg>
          <span>Đang tải ảnh bài chấm...</span>
        `;
      }
      img.classList.add("hidden");

      img.onload = () => {
        if (loading) loading.classList.add("hidden");
        img.classList.remove("hidden");
      };

      img.onerror = () => {
        if (!img.src.includes("/api/image/")) {
          img.src = `/api/image/${subId}?t=${Date.now()}`;
        } else {
          if (loading) loading.innerHTML = "⚠️ Không tìm thấy ảnh bài thi đã chấm.";
        }
      };

      img.src = `/api/graded-image/${subId}?t=${Date.now()}`;
      document.getElementById("imageViewerModal").classList.remove("hidden");
    }

    function closeImageViewer() {
      document.getElementById("imageViewerModal").classList.add("hidden");
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // QUẢN LÝ ĐỢT THI & NẠP ĐÁP ÁN (TAB 3)
    // =========================================================================
    function renderExamsList() {
      const container = document.getElementById("mobileExamsList");
      const allExams = sessionData.all_exams || [];
      const activeId = sessionData.active_exam_id;

      container.innerHTML = allExams.map(ex => {
        const isActive = (ex.id === activeId);
        return `
          <div onclick="selectExamMobile('${ex.id}')" class="p-2.5 rounded-xl border ${isActive ? 'bg-indigo-950/70 border-indigo-500 text-white' : 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-800 text-slate-300'} flex justify-between items-center text-xs">
            <div>
              <span class="font-bold">${ex.title}</span>
              <p class="text-[9px] text-slate-400">Đã chấm: ${ex.submission_count} bài</p>
            </div>
            ${isActive ? '<span class="text-[9px] bg-emerald-600 text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold">Đang chọn</span>' : ''}
          </div>
        `;
      }).join("");
    }

    async function selectExamMobile(examId) {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/select-exam/${examId}`, { method: "POST" });
        if (res.ok) {
          await refreshSessionData();
          renderExamsList();
          showNotificationToast("Đã chuyển sang đợt thi đã chọn.", false);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        showNotificationToast("Lỗi chuyển đợt thi: " + e.message, true);
      }
    }

    function toggleCreateExamForm() {
      const form = document.getElementById("formCreateExamMobile");
      form.classList.toggle("hidden");
    }

    async function submitCreateExamMobile() {
      const subject = document.getElementById("mobInpSubject").value.trim();
      const period = document.getElementById("mobInpPeriod").value.trim();
      const className = document.getElementById("mobInpClass").value.trim();

      if (!subject || !period || !className) {
        showNotificationToast("Vui lòng điền đủ Môn học, Kỳ thi và Lớp.", true);
        return;
      }

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/create-exam", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            subject: subject,
            period: period,
            class_name: className,
            school_year: "2026-2027"
          })
        });
        if (res.ok) {
          toggleCreateExamForm();
          await refreshSessionData();
          renderExamsList();
          showNotificationToast("Khởi tạo đợt thi mới thành công!", false);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        showNotificationToast("Lỗi khởi tạo đợt thi: " + e.message, true);
      }
    }

    async function uploadAnswerKeyMobile(e) {
      const file = e.target.files[0];
      if (!file) return;

      const fd = new FormData();
      fd.append("file", file);

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/upload-answer-key", { method: "POST", body: fd });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.status === "SUCCESS") {
          showNotificationToast(`✓ Đã nạp thành công ${data.exam_codes.length} mã đề!`, false);
          await refreshSessionData();
        } else {
          showNotificationToast("Lỗi nạp đáp án: " + (data.detail || "Không rõ"), true);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        showNotificationToast("Lỗi kết nối khi nạp đáp án: " + err.message, true);
      }
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // XUẤT FILE EXCEL & PDF TẢI VỀ HOẶC CHIA SẺ TRỰC TIẾP TỪ ĐIỆN THOẠI
    // =========================================================================
    async function exportExcelMobile() {
      const subs = sessionData.submissions || [];
      if (subs.length === 0) {
        showNotificationToast("Chưa có bài thi nào được chấm để xuất bảng điểm.", true);
        return;
      }

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/export-excel");
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Máy chủ báo lỗi khi tạo file Excel.");
        
        const blob = await res.blob();
        const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const a = document.createElement("a");
        a.href = url;
        a.download = `BANG_DIEM_${Date.now()}.xlsx`;
        document.body.appendChild(a);
        a.click();
        document.body.removeChild(a);
        window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      } catch (err) {
        showNotificationToast("Lỗi xuất Excel: " + err.message, true);
      }
    }

    function openSmartPasteMobile() {
      document.getElementById("mobSmartPasteText").value = "";
      const fb = document.getElementById("mobSmartPasteFeedback");
      fb.className = "hidden";
      fb.innerText = "";
      document.getElementById("mobileSmartPasteModal").classList.remove("hidden");
    }

    function closeSmartPasteMobile() {
      document.getElementById("mobileSmartPasteModal").classList.add("hidden");
    }

    async function submitSmartPasteMobile() {
      const text = document.getElementById("mobSmartPasteText").value.trim();
      const defaultMade = document.getElementById("mobDefaultMade").value.trim() || "0001";
      const fb = document.getElementById("mobSmartPasteFeedback");
      const btn = document.getElementById("btnMobSubmitSmartPaste");

      if (!text) {
        fb.className = "text-xs p-2 rounded bg-rose-950 text-rose-300 border border-rose-800 block";
        fb.innerText = "Vui lòng nhập hoặc dán nội dung đáp án.";
        return;
      }

      btn.disabled = true;
      btn.innerText = "Đang nạp...";
      fb.className = "text-xs p-2 rounded bg-indigo-950 text-indigo-300 border border-indigo-800 block";
      fb.innerText = "Đang nhận diện cú pháp...";

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/smart-paste-answer-key", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ text: text, default_made: defaultMade })
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.detail || "Lỗi xử lý đáp án");

        fb.className = "text-xs p-2 rounded bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-800 block";
        fb.innerHTML = `✓ Đã nạp mã đề: <b>${data.codes_loaded.join(", ")}</b>! Đã chấm lại ${data.re_graded_count} bài.`;
        showNotificationToast(`Đã nạp đáp án mã đề ${data.codes_loaded.join(", ")}!`, false);

        setTimeout(() => {
          closeSmartPasteMobile();
          syncSessionData();
        }, 1200);
      } catch (err) {
        fb.className = "text-xs p-2 rounded bg-rose-950 text-rose-300 border border-rose-800 block";
        fb.innerText = "Lỗi: " + err.message;
      } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.innerText = "Nạp Đáp Án";
      }
    }

    async function exportPdfMobile() {
      const subs = sessionData.submissions || [];
      if (subs.length === 0) {
        showNotificationToast("Chưa có bài thi nào được chấm để xuất PDF.", true);
        return;
      }

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/export-pdf");
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Máy chủ báo lỗi khi tạo file PDF.");

        const blob = await res.blob();
        const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const a = document.createElement("a");
        a.href = url;
        a.download = `TAP_BAI_CHAM_${Date.now()}.pdf`;
        document.body.appendChild(a);
        a.click();
        document.body.removeChild(a);
        window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      } catch (err) {
        showNotificationToast("Lỗi xuất PDF: " + err.message, true);
      }
    }
  </script>
</body>
</html>



=== FILE: templates\sw.js ===
// Service Worker cho OMR Mobile PWA
const CACHE_NAME = 'omr-pwa-v1';
const ASSETS_TO_CACHE = [
  '/scanner',
  '/manifest.json',
  '/icons/icon-192.png',
  '/icons/icon-512.png'
];

self.addEventListener('install', (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then((cache) => {
      return cache.addAll(ASSETS_TO_CACHE);
    }).then(() => self.skipWaiting())
  );
});

self.addEventListener('activate', (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches.keys().then((keys) => {
      return Promise.all(
        keys.filter((key) => key !== CACHE_NAME).map((key) => caches.delete(key))
      );
    }).then(() => self.clients.claim())
  );
});

// Network-first strategy cho API và Static fallback
self.addEventListener('fetch', (event) => {
  const url = new URL(event.request.url);

  // Không cache các lệnh POST hoặc các endpoint API chấm thi
  if (event.request.method !== 'GET' || url.pathname.startsWith('/api/')) {
    return;
  }

  event.respondWith(
    fetch(event.request)
      .then((response) => {
        // Lưu bản cache cho các tài nguyên tĩnh
        if (response.status === 200) {
          const resClone = response.clone();
          caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then((cache) => {
            cache.put(event.request, resClone);
          });
        }
        return response;
      })
      .catch(() => caches.match(event.request))
  );
});

=== FILE: tools\calibration\calibrate_header.py ===
# calibrate_header.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
HIEU CHUAN SO BAO DANH (8 COT) VA MA DE THI (4 COT)
Theo chuan phoi answer_sheet_master.png (1769 x 2526 px)
"""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import cv2
import numpy as np

IMAGE_PATH = Path("output/answer_sheet_master.png")
OUTPUT_DIR = Path("output")

# Quy cách:
# Số báo danh: 8 cột x 10 hàng (0..9) = 80 ô
# Mã đề thi: 4 cột x 10 hàng (0..9) = 40 ô
# Tổng cộng: 12 cột x 10 hàng = 120 ô

CLICK_LABELS = [
    # 1. Hàng chữ số 0 của SBD (8 điểm: Cột 1 đến Cột 8)
    "SBD - Chu so 0 - Cot 1",
    "SBD - Chu so 0 - Cot 2",
    "SBD - Chu so 0 - Cot 3",
    "SBD - Chu so 0 - Cot 4",
    "SBD - Chu so 0 - Cot 5",
    "SBD - Chu so 0 - Cot 6",
    "SBD - Chu so 0 - Cot 7",
    "SBD - Chu so 0 - Cot 8",
    # 2. Hàng chữ số 0 của Mã đề (4 điểm: Cột 1 đến Cột 4)
    "Ma de - Chu so 0 - Cot 1",
    "Ma de - Chu so 0 - Cot 2",
    "Ma de - Chu so 0 - Cot 3",
    "Ma de - Chu so 0 - Cot 4",
    # 3. Điểm đáy để tính khoảng cách bước nhảy hàng dọc (Hàng số 9)
    "SBD - Chu so 9 - Cot 1"
]

image = cv2.imread(str(IMAGE_PATH))
if image is None:
    raise FileNotFoundError(f"Khong tim thay anh: {IMAGE_PATH}")

height, width = image.shape[:2]

scale = min(1100 / width, 780 / height, 1.0)
display_width = int(width * scale)
display_height = int(height * scale)
scale_x = display_width / width
scale_y = display_height / height

display_original = cv2.resize(image, (display_width, display_height), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
display_image = display_original.copy()
points = []

WINDOW_NAME = "HIEU CHUAN SO BAO DANH VA MA DE"


def redraw():
    global display_image
    display_image = display_original.copy()
    for index, (x, y) in enumerate(points):
        px = int(round(x * scale_x))
        py = int(round(y * scale_y))
        cv2.circle(display_image, (px, py), 5, (0, 255, 0), 2)
        cv2.putText(
            display_image,
            str(index + 1),
            (px + 6, py - 6),
            cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
            0.4,
            (0, 0, 255),
            1
        )
    cv2.imshow(WINDOW_NAME, display_image)


def mouse_callback(event, x, y, flags, param):
    if event != cv2.EVENT_LBUTTONDOWN:
        return

    if len(points) >= len(CLICK_LABELS):
        print("Da chon du 13 diem. Nhan phím S de luu toa do.")
        return

    orig_x = int(round(x / scale_x))
    orig_y = int(round(y / scale_y))
    points.append((orig_x, orig_y))
    idx = len(points) - 1

    print(f"[{idx + 1}/13] {CLICK_LABELS[idx]}: x={orig_x}, y={orig_y}")
    redraw()

    if len(points) < len(CLICK_LABELS):
        print(f"--> Tiep theo: {CLICK_LABELS[len(points)]}")
    else:
        print("\n[✓] DA CHON DU 13 DIEM! Nhan phim S de luu, Backspace de xoa diem vua chon.")


def save_calibration():
    if len(points) != len(CLICK_LABELS):
        print(f"Chua du 13 diem: {len(points)}/13")
        return

    # 8 tọa độ X của SBD (Cột 1 đến 8)
    sbd_xs = [float(points[i][0]) for i in range(8)]
    # 4 tọa độ X của Mã đề (Cột 1 đến 4)
    made_xs = [float(points[i][0]) for i in range(8, 12)]

    # Hàng chữ số 0: lấy trung bình Y của 12 điểm chữ số 0
    row_zero_y = float(np.mean([points[i][1] for i in range(12)]))
    # Điểm chữ số 9 của cột 1
    row_nine_y = float(points[12][1])

    row_step = (row_nine_y - row_zero_y) / 9.0

    if row_step <= 10.0:
        raise ValueError(f"Khoang cach hang khong hop le: {row_step:.2f}")

    # Sinh danh sách tọa độ 10 hàng chữ số
    digits_y = [round(row_zero_y + d * row_step, 2) for d in range(10)]

    sbd_grid = []
    for col_idx, cx in enumerate(sbd_xs, start=1):
        col_data = {"column": col_idx, "x": round(cx, 2), "digits": {}}
        for d in range(10):
            col_data["digits"][str(d)] = [round(cx, 2), digits_y[d]]
        sbd_grid.append(col_data)

    made_grid = []
    for col_idx, cx in enumerate(made_xs, start=1):
        col_data = {"column": col_idx, "x": round(cx, 2), "digits": {}}
        for d in range(10):
            col_data["digits"][str(d)] = [round(cx, 2), digits_y[d]]
        made_grid.append(col_data)

    header_result = {
        "template": "answer_sheet_master",
        "template_width": width,
        "template_height": height,
        "row_step": round(row_step, 3),
        "digits_y_rows": digits_y,
        "sbd_columns": sbd_grid,
        "made_columns": made_grid
    }

    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    json_path = OUTPUT_DIR / "header_coordinates.json"
    with open(json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(header_result, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=4)

    # Vẽ kiểm tra 120 ô tròn lên ảnh mẫu
    preview = image.copy()
    for col in sbd_grid:
        for d, (x, y) in col["digits"].items():
            cv2.circle(preview, (int(x), int(y)), 8, (0, 200, 0), 2)
            cv2.putText(preview, d, (int(x) - 4, int(y) + 4), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.35, (0, 0, 255), 1)

    for col in made_grid:
        for d, (x, y) in col["digits"].items():
            cv2.circle(preview, (int(x), int(y)), 8, (255, 100, 0), 2)
            cv2.putText(preview, d, (int(x) - 4, int(y) + 4), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.35, (0, 0, 255), 1)

    preview_path = OUTPUT_DIR / "header_calibration_preview.png"
    cv2.imwrite(str(preview_path), preview)

    print("\n========================================================")
    print("HIEU CHUAN SO BAO DANH & MA DE THANH CONG!")
    print(f"- Khoang cach hang doc (row_step): {row_step:.2f} px")
    print(f"- File toa do da luu: {json_path.resolve()}")
    print(f"- Anh preview kiem tra: {preview_path.resolve()}")
    print("========================================================")


print("========================================================")
print("HUONG DAN HIEU CHUAN HEADER (SO BAO DANH & MA DE THI)")
print("========================================================")
for i, lbl in enumerate(CLICK_LABELS, start=1):
    print(f"{i:2d}. Click vao TAM o: {lbl}")
print("\n- Phim S: Luu toa do sau khi click du 13 diem")
print("- Phim Backspace: Xoa diem vua click nham")
print("- Phim ESC: Huy bo")

cv2.namedWindow(WINDOW_NAME, cv2.WINDOW_NORMAL)
cv2.resizeWindow(WINDOW_NAME, display_width, display_height)
cv2.setMouseCallback(WINDOW_NAME, mouse_callback)

while True:
    cv2.imshow(WINDOW_NAME, display_image)
    key = cv2.waitKey(20) & 0xFF
    if key == 27:
        print("Da huy.")
        break
    elif key in (8, 127):  # Backspace / Delete
        if points:
            removed = points.pop()
            print(f"Da xoa diem: {removed}")
            redraw()
    elif key == ord("s"):
        try:
            save_calibration()
            break
        except Exception as err:
            print("LOI:", err)

cv2.destroyAllWindows()


=== FILE: tools\calibration\calibrate_part1.py ===

import cv2
import json
import numpy as np
from pathlib import Path
import config

# ==========================================
# CAU HINH
# ==========================================

IMAGE_PATH = Path("output/answer_sheet_master.png")
OUTPUT_DIR = Path("output")

# Lay 4 toa do cau 1 da hieu chuan
FIRST_QUESTION = getattr(
    config,
    "PART1_Q1_POINTS",
    getattr(config, "ANSWER_POINTS", None)
)

if FIRST_QUESTION is None or len(FIRST_QUESTION) != 4:
    raise ValueError(
        "Config.py phai co PART1_Q1_POINTS hoac ANSWER_POINTS gom 4 toa do."
    )

FIRST_QUESTION = [
    tuple(map(float, point))
    for point in FIRST_QUESTION
]

# 13 diem can click them
LABELS = [
    "Cau 11 - A", "Cau 11 - B",
    "Cau 11 - C", "Cau 11 - D",

    "Cau 21 - A", "Cau 21 - B",
    "Cau 21 - C", "Cau 21 - D",

    "Cau 31 - A", "Cau 31 - B",
    "Cau 31 - C", "Cau 31 - D",

    "Cau 10 - A"
]

CHOICES = ["A", "B", "C", "D"]

# ==========================================
# DOC ANH
# ==========================================

image = cv2.imread(str(IMAGE_PATH))

if image is None:
    raise FileNotFoundError(
        f"Khong tim thay anh: {IMAGE_PATH}"
    )

height, width = image.shape[:2]

# Thu nho anh vua man hinh
scale = min(
    1050 / width,
    760 / height,
    1.0
)

display_width = int(width * scale)
display_height = int(height * scale)

scale_x = display_width / width
scale_y = display_height / height

display_image = cv2.resize(
    image,
    (display_width, display_height),
    interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA
)

# Danh sach 13 diem vua click
clicked_points = []

window_name = "HIEU CHUAN 40 CAU PHAN I"

# Ve 4 diem cau 1 da co san
for i, (x, y) in enumerate(FIRST_QUESTION):

    px = int(round(x * scale_x))
    py = int(round(y * scale_y))

    cv2.circle(
        display_image, (px, py), 6,
        (255, 0, 0), 2
    )

    cv2.putText(
        display_image,
        f"1{CHOICES[i]}",
        (px + 7, py - 5),
        cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
        0.4,
        (255, 0, 0),
        1
    )


def mouse_callback(event, x, y, flags, param):

    if event != cv2.EVENT_LBUTTONDOWN:
        return

    if len(clicked_points) >= len(LABELS):
        print("Da chon du 13 diem.")
        return

    # Chuyen toa do man hinh ve toa do anh goc
    original_x = int(round(x / scale_x))
    original_y = int(round(y / scale_y))

    clicked_points.append(
        (original_x, original_y)
    )

    index = len(clicked_points)
    label = LABELS[index - 1]

    cv2.circle(
        display_image,
        (x, y),
        6,
        (0, 255, 0),
        2
    )

    cv2.putText(
        display_image,
        str(index),
        (x + 7, y - 5),
        cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
        0.45,
        (0, 0, 255),
        1
    )

    cv2.imshow(window_name, display_image)

    print(
        f"{index}/13 - {label}: "
        f"x={original_x}, y={original_y}"
    )

    if index < len(LABELS):
        print("Tiep theo:", LABELS[index])


# ==========================================
# HUONG DAN VA HIEU CHUAN
# ==========================================

print("====================================")
print("HIEU CHUAN 40 CAU PHAN I")
print("====================================")
print("4 diem cua cau 1 da duoc nap san.")
print("Hay click vao TAM cac o tron theo thu tu:")
for i, label in enumerate(LABELS, start=1):
    print(f"{i}. {label}")

print("\nSau khi chon du 13 diem, nhan phim S de luu.")
print("Nhan ESC neu muon huy.")

cv2.namedWindow(
    window_name,
    cv2.WINDOW_NORMAL
)

cv2.resizeWindow(
    window_name,
    display_width,
    display_height
)

cv2.setMouseCallback(
    window_name,
    mouse_callback
)

while True:

    cv2.imshow(window_name, display_image)

    key = cv2.waitKey(20) & 0xFF

    if key == 27:
        print("Da huy hieu chuan.")
        break

    if key == ord("s"):

        if len(clicked_points) != len(LABELS):
            print(
                f"Chua du diem: "
                f"{len(clicked_points)}/13"
            )
            continue

        # ==================================
        # TINH KHOANG CACH CAC HANG
        # ==================================

        groups = [
            FIRST_QUESTION,
            clicked_points[0:4],
            clicked_points[4:8],
            clicked_points[8:12]
        ]

        y_starts = [
            float(np.mean([p[1] for p in group]))
            for group in groups
        ]

        # Cau 10 la hang thu 10 cua khoi dau
        y_step = (
            clicked_points[12][1] - y_starts[0]
        ) / 9

        if y_step <= 0:
            print("LOI: Khoang cach hang khong hop le.")
            continue

        # ==================================
        # TAO TOA DO 160 O
        # ==================================

        questions = []

        for block_index, group in enumerate(groups):

            x_positions = [
                float(point[0])
                for point in group
            ]

            for row in range(10):

                question_number = (
                    block_index * 10 + row + 1
                )

                y = y_starts[block_index] + row * y_step

                choices = {}

                for choice_index, choice in enumerate(CHOICES):

                    choices[choice] = [
                        round(x_positions[choice_index], 2),
                        round(y, 2)
                    ]

                questions.append({
                    "question": question_number,
                    "choices": choices
                })

        result = {
            "template": "answer_sheet_v1",
            "template_width": width,
            "template_height": height,
            "row_step": round(y_step, 3),
            "questions": questions
        }

        OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

        json_path = OUTPUT_DIR / "part1_coordinates.json"

        with open(json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as file:
            json.dump(
                result,
                file,
                ensure_ascii=False,
                indent=4
            )

        # ==================================
        # VE KIEM TRA 160 TOA DO
        # ==================================

        preview = image.copy()

        for question in questions:

            q = question["question"]

            for choice, point in question["choices"].items():

                x, y = map(int, point)

                cv2.circle(
                    preview,
                    (x, y),
                    7,
                    (0, 180, 0),
                    2
                )

                cv2.putText(
                    preview,
                    str(q),
                    (x + 7, y - 5),
                    cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
                    0.3,
                    (0, 0, 255),
                    1
                )

        preview_path = OUTPUT_DIR / "part1_calibration_preview.png"

        cv2.imwrite(
            str(preview_path),
            preview
        )

        print("\nHIEU CHUAN THANH CONG!")
        print("So cau:", len(questions))
        print("So o dap an:", len(questions) * 4)
        print("Khoang cach hang:", round(y_step, 2))
        print("File toa do:", json_path.resolve())
        print("Anh kiem tra:", preview_path.resolve())

        break

cv2.destroyAllWindows()



=== FILE: tools\calibration\calibrate_part2.py ===

import cv2
import json
from pathlib import Path

# ==========================================
# CAU HINH
# ==========================================

IMAGE_PATH = Path("output/answer_sheet_master.png")
OUTPUT_DIR = Path("output")

ROWS = ["a", "b", "c", "d"]

# Tao danh sach 17 diem can click
CLICK_LABELS = [
    "Cau 1 - y a - DUNG",
    "Cau 1 - y a - SAI",
    "Cau 1 - y d - DUNG",
]

for q in range(2, 9):
    CLICK_LABELS.extend([
        f"Cau {q} - y a - DUNG",
        f"Cau {q} - y a - SAI",
    ])

# ==========================================
# DOC ANH PHIEU MAU
# ==========================================

image = cv2.imread(str(IMAGE_PATH))

if image is None:
    raise FileNotFoundError(
        f"Khong tim thay anh: {IMAGE_PATH}"
    )

height, width = image.shape[:2]

# Tu dong thu nho anh vua cua so
screen_width = 1100
screen_height = 800

scale = min(
    screen_width / width,
    screen_height / height,
    1.0
)

display_width = int(width * scale)
display_height = int(height * scale)

scale_x = display_width / width
scale_y = display_height / height

display_image = cv2.resize(
    image,
    (display_width, display_height),
    interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA
)

original_display = display_image.copy()

points = []

WINDOW_NAME = "HIEU CHUAN PHAN II"


# ==========================================
# VE CAC DIEM DA CHON
# ==========================================

def redraw():

    global display_image

    display_image = original_display.copy()

    for index, (x, y) in enumerate(points):

        px = int(round(x * scale_x))
        py = int(round(y * scale_y))

        cv2.circle(
            display_image,
            (px, py),
            5,
            (0, 255, 0),
            2
        )

        cv2.putText(
            display_image,
            str(index + 1),
            (px + 5, py - 5),
            cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
            0.4,
            (0, 0, 255),
            1
        )

    cv2.imshow(WINDOW_NAME, display_image)


# ==========================================
# XU LY CLICK CHUOT
# ==========================================

def mouse_callback(event, x, y, flags, param):

    if event != cv2.EVENT_LBUTTONDOWN:
        return

    if len(points) >= len(CLICK_LABELS):
        print("Da chon du 17 diem.")
        return

    # Chuyen toa do hien thi ve toa do anh goc
    original_x = int(round(x / scale_x))
    original_y = int(round(y / scale_y))

    points.append((original_x, original_y))

    index = len(points) - 1

    print(
        f"{index + 1}/17 - "
        f"{CLICK_LABELS[index]}: "
        f"x={original_x}, y={original_y}"
    )

    redraw()

    if len(points) < len(CLICK_LABELS):
        print(
            "Tiep theo:",
            CLICK_LABELS[len(points)]
        )
    else:
        print("\nDA CHON DU 17 DIEM!")
        print("Nhan S de luu toa do.")


# ==========================================
# TAO 64 TOA DO
# ==========================================

def generate_coordinates():

    # Hai diem dau la True va False cua y a cau 1
    q1_true = points[0]
    q1_false = points[1]

    # Diem thu ba la True cua y d cau 1
    q1_last = points[2]

    # Tinh toa do trung binh hang a
    q1_y = (q1_true[1] + q1_false[1]) / 2

    # Khoang cach giua cac hang
    row_step = (q1_last[1] - q1_y) / 3

    if not (10 <= row_step <= 150):
        raise ValueError(
            "Khoang cach hang khong hop le. "
            "Hay kiem tra lai diem cau 1."
        )

    questions = []

    # Cau 1
    anchors = {
        1: {
            "true_x": q1_true[0],
            "false_x": q1_false[0],
            "start_y": q1_y
        }
    }

    # Cau 2 den cau 8
    index = 3

    for q in range(2, 9):

        true_point = points[index]
        false_point = points[index + 1]

        index += 2

        if abs(true_point[1] - false_point[1]) > 12:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Hai diem Dung/Sai cau {q} "
                "khong cung hang. Hay hieu chuan lai."
            )

        anchors[q] = {
            "true_x": true_point[0],
            "false_x": false_point[0],
            "start_y": (
                true_point[1] + false_point[1]
            ) / 2
        }

    # Sinh 8 cau x 4 y x 2 lua chon = 64 o
    for q in range(1, 9):

        anchor = anchors[q]

        question_data = {
            "question": q,
            "rows": {}
        }

        for row_index, row_label in enumerate(ROWS):

            y = (
                anchor["start_y"]
                + row_index * row_step
            )

            question_data["rows"][row_label] = {
                "true": [
                    round(float(anchor["true_x"]), 2),
                    round(float(y), 2)
                ],
                "false": [
                    round(float(anchor["false_x"]), 2),
                    round(float(y), 2)
                ]
            }

        questions.append(question_data)

    return questions, row_step


# ==========================================
# LUU TOA DO VA ANH KIEM TRA
# ==========================================

def save_calibration():

    if len(points) != 17:
        print("Chua chon du 17 diem.")
        return

    questions, row_step = generate_coordinates()

    result = {
        "template": "answer_sheet_v1",
        "template_width": width,
        "template_height": height,
        "row_step": round(row_step, 3),
        "questions": questions
    }

    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    json_path = OUTPUT_DIR / "part2_coordinates.json"

    with open(
        json_path,
        "w",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:

        json.dump(
            result,
            f,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=4
        )

    # Ve 64 vi tri len anh mau
    preview = image.copy()

    for question in questions:

        q = question["question"]

        for row_label in ROWS:

            row = question["rows"][row_label]

            for choice in ["true", "false"]:

                x, y = row[choice]

                x = int(round(x))
                y = int(round(y))

                cv2.circle(
                    preview,
                    (x, y),
                    9,
                    (0, 180, 0),
                    2
                )

                label = (
                    str(q) + row_label
                    + ("T" if choice == "true" else "S")
                )

                cv2.putText(
                    preview,
                    label,
                    (x + 8, y - 4),
                    cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
                    0.25,
                    (0, 0, 255),
                    1
                )

    preview_path = (
        OUTPUT_DIR / "part2_calibration_preview.png"
    )

    cv2.imwrite(
        str(preview_path),
        preview
    )

    print("\n================================")
    print("HIEU CHUAN PHAN II THANH CONG!")
    print("So cau:", len(questions))
    print("So o:", len(questions) * 4 * 2)
    print("Khoang cach hang:", round(row_step, 2))
    print("File toa do:", json_path.resolve())
    print("Anh kiem tra:", preview_path.resolve())
    print("================================")


# ==========================================
# CHUONG TRINH CHINH
# ==========================================

print("HUONG DAN HIEU CHUAN PHAN II")
print("--------------------------------")

for i, label in enumerate(CLICK_LABELS, start=1):
    print(f"{i}. {label}")

print("\nClick dung TAM cac o tron.")
print("S: Luu khi da chon du 17 diem.")
print("ESC: Huy.")

cv2.namedWindow(
    WINDOW_NAME,
    cv2.WINDOW_NORMAL
)

cv2.resizeWindow(
    WINDOW_NAME,
    display_width,
    display_height
)

cv2.setMouseCallback(
    WINDOW_NAME,
    mouse_callback
)

while True:

    cv2.imshow(WINDOW_NAME, display_image)

    key = cv2.waitKey(20) & 0xFF

    if key == 27:
        print("Da huy hieu chuan.")
        break

    if key == ord("s"):

        try:
            save_calibration()
            break

        except Exception as error:
            print("LOI:", error)

cv2.destroyAllWindows()



=== FILE: tools\calibration\calibrate_part3.py ===

import cv2
import json
from pathlib import Path

# ==========================================
# CAU HINH
# ==========================================

IMAGE_PATH = Path("output/answer_sheet_master.png")
OUTPUT_DIR = Path("output")

QUESTION_COUNT = 6
DIGITS = list(range(10))
DIGIT_COLUMNS = 4

# ==========================================
# TAO DANH SACH 28 DIEM CLICK
# ==========================================

CLICK_LABELS = [
    "Cau 1 - Chu so 0 - Cot 1",
    "Cau 1 - Chu so 0 - Cot 2",
    "Cau 1 - Chu so 0 - Cot 3",
    "Cau 1 - Chu so 0 - Cot 4",

    "Cau 1 - Chu so 9 - Cot 1",

    "Cau 1 - Dau am",
    "Cau 1 - Dau phay vi tri 1",
    "Cau 1 - Dau phay vi tri 2",
]

for q in range(2, 7):
    for col in range(1, 5):
        CLICK_LABELS.append(
            f"Cau {q} - Chu so 0 - Cot {col}"
        )

# ==========================================
# DOC ANH PHIEU MAU
# ==========================================

image = cv2.imread(str(IMAGE_PATH))

if image is None:
    raise FileNotFoundError(
        f"Khong tim thay anh: {IMAGE_PATH}"
    )

height, width = image.shape[:2]

# Thu nho anh vua man hinh
scale = min(
    760 / height,
    1100 / width,
    1.0
)

display_width = int(width * scale)
display_height = int(height * scale)

scale_x = display_width / width
scale_y = display_height / height

display_original = cv2.resize(
    image,
    (display_width, display_height),
    interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA
)

display_image = display_original.copy()

points = []

WINDOW_NAME = "HIEU CHUAN PHAN III"

# ==========================================
# VE CAC DIEM DA CHON
# ==========================================

def redraw():

    global display_image

    display_image = display_original.copy()

    for index, (x, y) in enumerate(points):

        px = int(round(x * scale_x))
        py = int(round(y * scale_y))

        cv2.circle(
            display_image,
            (px, py),
            5,
            (0, 255, 0),
            2
        )

        cv2.putText(
            display_image,
            str(index + 1),
            (px + 5, py - 5),
            cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
            0.4,
            (0, 0, 255),
            1
        )

    cv2.imshow(WINDOW_NAME, display_image)


# ==========================================
# CLICK CHUOT
# ==========================================

def mouse_callback(event, x, y, flags, param):

    if event != cv2.EVENT_LBUTTONDOWN:
        return

    if len(points) >= len(CLICK_LABELS):
        print("Da chon du 28 diem.")
        return

    original_x = int(round(x / scale_x))
    original_y = int(round(y / scale_y))

    points.append((original_x, original_y))

    index = len(points) - 1

    print(
        f"{index + 1}/28 - "
        f"{CLICK_LABELS[index]}: "
        f"x={original_x}, y={original_y}"
    )

    redraw()

    if len(points) < len(CLICK_LABELS):
        print(
            "Tiep theo:",
            CLICK_LABELS[len(points)]
        )
    else:
        print("\nDA CHON DU 28 DIEM!")
        print("Nhan S de luu toa do.")


# ==========================================
# KIEM TRA VA TAO TOA DO
# ==========================================

def save_calibration():

    if len(points) != 28:
        print("Chua chon du 28 diem.")
        return

    # Toa do hang chu so 0 va 9 cua cau 1
    row_zero = points[0][1]
    row_nine = points[4][1]

    row_step = (row_nine - row_zero) / 9

    if not (12 <= row_step <= 80):
        raise ValueError(
            "Khoang cach hang khong hop le."
        )

    # Dau am va dau phay
    sign_y = points[5][1]

    comma_y1 = points[6][1]
    comma_y2 = points[7][1]

    if abs(comma_y1 - comma_y2) > 12:
        raise ValueError(
            "Hai diem dau phay khong cung hang."
        )

    comma_y = (comma_y1 + comma_y2) / 2

    # Lay 4 toa do cot chu so cua 6 cau
    x_groups = []

    # Cau 1
    x_groups.append([
        points[i][0]
        for i in range(4)
    ])

    # Cau 2 den cau 6
    index = 8

    for q in range(2, 7):

        xs = [
            points[index + i][0]
            for i in range(4)
        ]

        index += 4

        x_groups.append(xs)

    # Kiem tra cac cot tang dan
    for q, xs in enumerate(x_groups, start=1):

        if not all(
            xs[i] < xs[i + 1]
            for i in range(3)
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                f"Toa do cot chu so cau {q} khong hop le."
            )

    # Kiem tra vi tri dau am va dau phay
    ref_xs = x_groups[0]

    if abs(points[5][0] - ref_xs[0]) > 20:
        raise ValueError(
            "Vi tri dau am khong dung cot 1."
        )

    if abs(points[6][0] - ref_xs[1]) > 20:
        raise ValueError(
            "Dau phay vi tri 1 khong dung."
        )

    if abs(points[7][0] - ref_xs[2]) > 20:
        raise ValueError(
            "Dau phay vi tri 2 khong dung."
        )

    # ======================================
    # SINH TOA DO 6 CAU
    # ======================================

    questions = []

    for q in range(QUESTION_COUNT):

        xs = x_groups[q]

        question_data = {
            "question": q + 1,

            "sign": [
                round(float(xs[0]), 2),
                round(float(sign_y), 2)
            ],

            "comma_positions": [
                [
                    round(float(xs[1]), 2),
                    round(float(comma_y), 2)
                ],
                [
                    round(float(xs[2]), 2),
                    round(float(comma_y), 2)
                ]
            ],

            "digits": {}
        }

        for digit in DIGITS:

            y = row_zero + digit * row_step

            question_data["digits"][str(digit)] = [
                [
                    round(float(x), 2),
                    round(float(y), 2)
                ]
                for x in xs
            ]

        questions.append(question_data)

    result = {
        "template": "answer_sheet_v1",
        "template_width": width,
        "template_height": height,
        "row_step": round(row_step, 3),
        "questions": questions
    }

    # ======================================
    # LUU JSON
    # ======================================

    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    json_path = OUTPUT_DIR / "part3_coordinates.json"

    with open(
        json_path,
        "w",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:

        json.dump(
            result,
            f,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=4
        )

    # ======================================
    # VE ANH KIEM TRA
    # ======================================

    preview = image.copy()

    for question in questions:

        # Dau am - xanh duong
        x, y = question["sign"]

        cv2.circle(
            preview,
            (int(x), int(y)),
            8,
            (255, 0, 0),
            2
        )

        # Dau phay - mau vang
        for x, y in question["comma_positions"]:

            cv2.circle(
                preview,
                (int(x), int(y)),
                8,
                (0, 165, 255),
                2
            )

        # 40 o chu so cua moi cau - xanh la
        for digit, positions in question["digits"].items():

            for x, y in positions:

                cv2.circle(
                    preview,
                    (int(x), int(y)),
                    7,
                    (0, 180, 0),
                    2
                )

    preview_path = (
        OUTPUT_DIR / "part3_calibration_preview.png"
    )

    cv2.imwrite(
        str(preview_path),
        preview
    )

    print("\n================================")
    print("HIEU CHUAN PHAN III THANH CONG!")
    print("So cau:", len(questions))
    print("So o chu so:", 240)
    print("So dau am:", 6)
    print("So vi tri dau phay:", 12)
    print("Khoang cach hang:", round(row_step, 2))
    print("File toa do:", json_path.resolve())
    print("Anh kiem tra:", preview_path.resolve())
    print("================================")


# ==========================================
# CHUONG TRINH CHINH
# ==========================================

print("HIEU CHUAN PHAN III")
print("--------------------------------")

for i, label in enumerate(CLICK_LABELS, start=1):
    print(f"{i}. {label}")

print("\nClick vao TAM cac o tron.")
print("S: Luu toa do khi da chon du 28 diem.")
print("Backspace: Xoa diem vua chon.")
print("ESC: Huy.")

cv2.namedWindow(
    WINDOW_NAME,
    cv2.WINDOW_NORMAL
)

cv2.resizeWindow(
    WINDOW_NAME,
    display_width,
    display_height
)

cv2.setMouseCallback(
    WINDOW_NAME,
    mouse_callback
)

while True:

    cv2.imshow(WINDOW_NAME, display_image)

    key = cv2.waitKey(20) & 0xFF

    if key == 27:
        print("Da huy.")
        break

    elif key in (8, 127):

        if points:
            removed = points.pop()
            print("Da xoa diem:", removed)
            redraw()

    elif key == ord("s"):

        try:
            save_calibration()
            break

        except Exception as error:
            print("LOI:", error)

cv2.destroyAllWindows()

=== FILE: tools\calibration\convert_pdf.py ===

import fitz
from pathlib import Path

# Tim file PDF mau trong thu muc du an
pdf_files = list(Path(".").glob("*.pdf"))

if not pdf_files:
    print("LOI: Khong tim thay file PDF mau!")
else:
    pdf_path = pdf_files[0]
    output_dir = Path("output")
    output_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    print("Dang doc file:", pdf_path.name)

    # Mo file PDF
    document = fitz.open(pdf_path)

    # Chi xu ly trang dau tien
    page = document[0]

    # Tang do phan giai anh len 3 lan
    matrix = fitz.Matrix(3, 3)

    # Chuyen trang PDF thanh anh
    pixmap = page.get_pixmap(
        matrix=matrix,
        alpha=False
    )

    # Luu anh PNG
    output_path = output_dir / "answer_sheet_master.png"
    pixmap.save(output_path)

    print("--------------------------------")
    print("CHUYEN DOI THANH CONG!")
    print("So trang PDF:", len(document))
    print("Kich thuoc anh:", pixmap.width, "x", pixmap.height)
    print("Anh duoc luu tai:", output_path.resolve())
    print("--------------------------------")

    document.close()
